IKSolverType
Perlin
Classic perlin 2-bone solver
2-bone solver that does not have singularities that Perlin does, and should be used as a default starting point for 2 bone solves.
FABRIK
"Forward And Backward Reaching Inverse Kinematics" solver - A solver that can handle any number of bones and works by iteratively solving for the position of each bone in the chain.
Dog Leg (3-Bone)
A 3-bone solver that uses two 2-bone solves under the hood to emulate a dog leg.
Cyclic Coordinate Descent solver - A solver that can handle any number of bones and works by iteratively solving for the rotation of each bone in the chain.
IKSOLVER_Perlin
IKSOLVER_TwoBone
IKSOLVER_Fabrik
IKSOLVER_DogLeg3Bone
IKSOLVER_CCD
IKSOLVER_COUNT
EIKEndEffectorRotationFixUpMode
However the end effector ends up after the solve, it's rotation will remain unchanged.
Match Target Orientation
The targets orientation will be stamped onto the end effector.
Look At Target Forward
The targets forward vector will be used to build a look orientation while preserving the rotation of the end effector after the solve as much as possible.
Maintain Parent Orientation
Use the parent bone's orientation as the end effector's orientation.
MatchTargetOrientation
LookAtTargetForward
MaintainParentOrientation

Remove Tag From Cursor
Remove the given tag from this cursor. If other cursors are waiting on us, they will resume.
Any extra waiting cursors will be terminated. -1 for infinite cursors.
